<style>.opb-evidence .pp-note { background:#f2f5fa; border-left:3px solid #526f9e; padding:10px 12px; }
.opb-evidence details { color:#475c7a; }
.opb-evidence summary { cursor:pointer; padding:8px 0; }

.opb-missing { max-width:240px; white-space:normal; }

<script>
/* 同SKU自身补证只填原利润缺口；完整历史成本与调价模型继续使用原实现。 */
(function (root) {
  'use strict';
  const packet = JSON.parse(document.getElementById('allSkuOwnProfitData').textContent);
  const model = root.ppProfitModel;
  if (!model?.ready || packet.status !== 'verified') throw Error('全SKU自身利润补证尚未验收');
  const finite = value => typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value);
  const esc = value => cxEsc(String(value ?? ''));
  const records = new Map((packet.rows || []).map(row => [row.sku, row]));
  const samplesBySku = new Map(), cache = new Map();
  const orderKey = row => [row.platform, row.store, row.orderId, row.sku].join('|');
  for (const sample of packet.samples || []) {
    if (!records.has(sample.sku) || sample.sourceSku !== sample.sku || sample.eligibleForMargin !== true
      || !(sample.quantity > 0 && sample.revenue > 0) || !finite(sample.pureOperatingProfitTotal)
      || !finite(sample.pureTrueProfitTotal) || !(sample.truePurchase > 0)
      || !finite(sample.operatingPurchase) || Math.abs(sample.operatingPurchase - sample.truePurchase * 1.5) > 1e-7
      || !['head', 'tariff', 'handling', 'tail', 'platformFee', 'advertising', 'consumptionTax', 'installation', 'aftersalesReserve']
        .every(key => finite(sample.componentTotals?.[key])) || !sample.sourceLineage?.length) throw Error('补证样本身份或利润无效：' + sample.sku);
    if (!samplesBySku.has(sample.sku)) samplesBySku.set(sample.sku, new Map());
    const group = samplesBySku.get(sample.sku), key = orderKey(sample);
    if (group.has(key)) throw Error('补证样本重复：' + key);
    group.set(key, sample);
  }
  const recentFirst = (a, b) => {
    for (const key of ['sampleDate', 'platform', 'store', 'orderId', 'key']) {
      const left = String(a[key] || ''), right = String(b[key] || '');
      if (left !== right) return left < right ? 1 : -1;
    }
    return 0;
  };
  for (const [sku, group] of samplesBySku) samplesBySku.set(sku, [...group.values()].sort(recentFirst));
  function decorate(sample) {
    const storage = model.storageFor(sample), fee = storage.storage?.representative;
    const factor = 1 + (sample.aftersalesRate ?? 0.05), reduction = finite(fee) ? factor * fee * sample.quantity : null;
    return { ...sample, storageScenario: storage, storageFee: finite(fee) ? fee : null,
      storageFeeLower: storage.storage?.lower ?? null, storageFeeUpper: storage.storage?.upper ?? null,
      storageOperatingProfitTotal: finite(reduction) ? sample.pureOperatingProfitTotal - reduction : null,
      storageTrueProfitTotal: finite(reduction) ? sample.pureTrueProfitTotal - reduction : null,
      storageOperatingMargin: finite(reduction) ? (sample.pureOperatingProfitTotal - reduction) / sample.revenue : null,
      storageTrueMargin: finite(reduction) ? (sample.pureTrueProfitTotal - reduction) / sample.revenue : null,
      storageReserveDeltaTotal: finite(fee) ? (factor - 1) * fee * sample.quantity : null };
  }
  const storeName = (platform, value) => sqStoreName(platform, value).toLowerCase();
  function forecastFor(samples, scenarios) {
    const projected = [];
    for (const sample of samples) {
      const matching = scenarios.filter(row => row.platform === sample.platform
        && storeName(row.platform, row.store) === storeName(sample.platform, sample.store)
        && (!row.shop_sku || row.shop_sku.toUpperCase() === String(sample.shopSku || sample.sku).toUpperCase())
        && (!(sample.listingId || sample.asin || sample.listing_id) || row.listing_id === (sample.listingId || sample.asin || sample.listing_id)));
      if (!matching.length || new Set(matching.map(row => row.after_total)).size !== 1) continue;
      const row = model.reprice(sample, matching[0]);
      if (row) projected.push(decorate(row));
      if (projected.length === 20) break;
    }
    return projected;
  }
  const priorForSku = model.forSku;
  model.forSku = function (sku, filters = {}) {
    const base = priorForSku(sku, filters), evidence = records.get(sku);
    if (!evidence) return base;
    // 不以较弱的补证估算替换原来已有的完整成本或BTZ已验收样本。
    if (base.selected) return base;
    const key = JSON.stringify([sku, filters.platform || '', filters.store || '', filters.evidenceMode || 'current']);
    if (cache.has(key)) return cache.get(key);
    const eligible = (samplesBySku.get(sku) || []).filter(row => model.channelMatches(row, filters));
    const historicalSamples = eligible.slice(0, 20).map(decorate), historical = model.aggregate(historicalSamples);
    const forecastSamples = filters.evidenceMode === 'withHistory' ? [] : forecastFor(eligible, base.scenarios || []);
    const forecast = model.aggregate(forecastSamples), selected = forecast || historical;
    const newRejected = (evidence.rejectedSamples || []).map(row => ({ ...row, sku })).filter(row => model.channelMatches(row, filters));
    const validKeys = new Set(eligible.map(orderKey));
    const rejected = [...new Map([...(base.rejected || []), ...newRejected]
      .filter(row => !validKeys.has(orderKey(row))).map(row => [orderKey(row), row])).values()];
    const noChannelSample = !selected && (samplesBySku.get(sku) || []).length > 0;
    const missingFields = selected ? evidence.missingFields || [] : noChannelSample ? ['当前平台／店铺无可用自身成本样本；其他渠道样本未混入']
      : evidence.missingFields?.length ? evidence.missingFields : ['尚未取得此SKU自身完整收入、采购与费用证据'];
    const result = { ...base, historical, forecast, selected,
      samples: forecast ? forecastSamples : historicalSamples, historicalSamples,
      validSampleCount: eligible.length, rejectedSampleCount: rejected.length, rejected,
      status: forecast ? '通知价 · 自身成本预估' : selected ? '同SKU自身成本预估' : noChannelSample ? '当前渠道无自身样本' : '自身证据未闭合',
      isForecast: Boolean(forecast), isEstimate: true, isOwnCostEstimate: Boolean(selected),
      ownBackfillEvidence: { ...evidence, missingFields }, ownBackfill: true, noChannelSample, noCrossSkuFallback: true };
    cache.set(key, result); return result;
  };
  const priorClear = model.clearCaches;
  model.clearCaches = function () { cache.clear(); return priorClear.apply(this, arguments); };
  const priorBuild = sqBuildRow;
  sqBuildRow = function (sku, filters) {
    const row = priorBuild(sku, filters), profit = row._profit20;
    if (!profit?.ownBackfill) return row;
    const e = profit.ownBackfillEvidence, s = profit.selected;
    return { ...row, profitBasis: s ? profit.isForecast ? 'own_cost_notice_forecast' : 'same_sku_own_cost_estimate' : 'missing',
      profitBackfillStatus: profit.status, profitMissingFields: (e.missingFields || []).join('；'),
      profitEstimationNotes: [...new Set((profit.samples || []).flatMap(sample => sample.notes || []))].join('；'),
      sourceNote: s ? '同SKU自身成本预估；样本 ' + s.periodStart + '—' + s.periodEnd + '，' + s.sampleCount
        + '单，' + s.platformCount + '平台／' + s.storeCount + '店铺；按收入加权，未含累计仓储。费用估算与原始依据见逐单详情。'
        : '仅核本SKU历史订单、原采购及账单；仍缺：' + (e.missingFields || ['完整自身成本']).join('；') };
  };
  // 销售页利润分层与关注清单共用同一补证结果；非库存独立业务仍按原范围门禁。
  const priorLeadershipReference = ldReference;
  ldReference = function (sku, ignored = '') {
    if (!samplesBySku.has(sku)) return priorLeadershipReference.apply(this, arguments);
    if (['nonstock', 'pending'].includes(cxState.sales.stockScope)
      || cxState.sales.stockScope === 'business' && ['nonstock', 'pending'].includes(sxState.stockClass)) return null;
    if (cfState.profitMode === 'current') return priorLeadershipReference.apply(this, arguments);
    const filters = { ...cfReferenceScope(ignored), evidenceMode: 'withHistory' };
    const profit = model.forSku(sku, filters), summary = profit.selected;
    if (!summary) return null;
    return { ...(priorLeadershipReference.apply(this, arguments) || {}), sku,
      key: 'own-cost-recent20|' + sku + '|' + (filters.platform || '') + '|' + (filters.store || ''),
      operatingMargin: summary.operatingMargin, trueMargin: summary.trueMargin,
      eligibleForMargin: true, basisType: 'historical', isEstimate: true, ownBackfill: true,
      platform: summary.platformCount === 1 ? summary.platforms[0] : '多平台',
      store: summary.stores.join('；'), priceDate: summary.periodStart + '—' + summary.periodEnd,
      sourceNote: '同SKU最近' + summary.sampleCount + '单自身成本预估；未含累计仓储；费用模型见逐单详情' };
  };
  const priorProfitCharts = ldProfitCharts;
  ldProfitCharts = function () {
    const result = priorProfitCharts.apply(this, arguments);
    if (['nonstock', 'pending'].includes(cxState.sales.stockScope)
      || cxState.sales.stockScope === 'business' && ['nonstock', 'pending'].includes(sxState.stockClass)) return result;
    return { ...result, html: result.html + '<p class="cx-note opb-chart-note">本轮补证SKU采用同SKU最近最多20笔可核成本订单综合，按收入加权，未含累计仓储；标记为自身成本预估，来源与费用假设可在SKU“利润与调价”中展开。仅当前价格模式不引入历史补证。</p>' };
  };
  const priorCell = sqUiCell;
  sqUiCell = function (column, row) {
    const html = priorCell(column, row), profit = row._profit20;
    if (!profit?.ownBackfill) return html;
    if (column === 'operatingMargin' || column === 'realMargin') return html.replace(/最近\d+单综合/g, '同SKU自身成本预估');
    if (column === 'evidence') return html.replace('pp-status ', 'pp-status estimate ')
      + '<small class="pp-sub opb-missing">' + esc(profit.selected ? '原采购与费用来源已匹配；估算项逐单披露'
        : (profit.ownBackfillEvidence.missingFields || []).join('；')) + '</small>';
    if (column === 'scope') return '<small class="pp-sub">报价原引用</small>'
      + html.replace('利润参考仓：', '报价原参考仓：')
      + '<small class="pp-sub">利润样本来源：' + esc(profit.selected?.stores?.join('；') || '当前平台／店铺无可用自身样本') + '</small>';
    return html;
  };
  function evidenceHtml(sku, filters) {
    const result = model.forSku(sku, filters), e = result.ownBackfillEvidence;
    if (!e) return '';
    const samples = result.samples || [];
    const notes = [...new Set([...(e.notes || []), ...samples.flatMap(row => row.notes || [])])];
    return '<section class="pp-section opb-evidence"><h3>同SKU历史订单与采购补证</h3>'
      + '<p class="pp-note">' + esc(result.selected ? '本次补齐采用本SKU自己的历史销售、采购与费用依据；费用模型和当前仓储模拟均单独标注。此处为成本预估，不是财务实结利润。'
        : '已检索本SKU自己的历史订单、采购登记及物流账单；现有证据尚不能闭合全部必要成本，不使用其他SKU补值。') + '</p>'
      + '<details><summary>查看本次核对范围、估算口径与剩余缺项</summary>'
      + '<p>' + esc('自身历史候选 ' + (e.candidateCount || 0) + '单；有可用成本依据 '
        + result.validSampleCount + '单。按所选平台／店铺先筛后取最近最多20单，销量日期不截断成本历史。') + '</p>'
      + (!result.selected ? '<p><strong>仍缺：</strong>' + esc((e.missingFields || []).join('；')) + '</p>' : '')
      + notes.map(note => '<p>' + esc(note) + '</p>').join('')
      + '<p>运营采购＝原真实采购×1.5；两种利润采用同一收入、其他费用及售后储备分别重算。缺失费用不按0，重复账单和未明确分摊的混合订单不强行计入。</p>'
      + '</details></section>';
  }
  function currentFilters() {
    return cxState.view === 'query' ? sqUiState.filters : { platform: cxState.sales?.platform || '', store: sxState?.store || '' };
  }
  function sampleEvidenceHtml(sample) {
    const purchase = sample.purchaseEvidence || {}, tail = sample.tailEvidence || {}, volume = sample.volumeEvidence || {};
    const money = value => finite(value) ? cxMoney(value) : '未取得';
    const reference = [tail.sourceOrderId || sample.orderId, tail.referenceDate || '',
      tail.platform ? (tail.platform === 'Shopify' ? '独立站' : tail.platform) : '', tail.store || ''].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
    const lines = [
      '采购原价：' + (purchase.trueCnyUnit ?? '未取得') + ' ' + (purchase.purchaseCurrency || sample.sourcePurchaseCurrency || '')
        + '/件；采购参考日期：' + (purchase.referenceDates?.join('、') || '原记录日期未核')
        + '；汇率参数：1 USD＝' + (purchase.modelFx ?? sample.sourcePurchaseFxCnyPerUsd ?? '未核') + ' CNY（非实付汇率）。',
      '尾程' + (tail.actual ? '本单费用依据：' : '估算参考：') + reference
        + (finite(tail.tailPerUnit) ? '；参考每件 ' + money(tail.tailPerUnit) : '')
        + '；本次计入 ' + money(sample.componentTotals.tail) + (tail.actual ? '。' : '，不称本单实际尾程。'),
      '自身总体积：' + (finite(volume.totalCbm) ? Number(volume.totalCbm.toFixed(6)) + ' m³' : '未取得')
        + (volume.basis === 'same_order_complete_packages' ? '；同订单完整包件。' : '；同SKU自身尺寸参考，非本单实际测量。'),
      '作业费模型 ' + money(sample.handlingModelTotal) + '；已证出库作业 ' + money(sample.actualOutboundHandlingControlTotal)
        + '；本次计入 ' + money(sample.componentTotals.handling) + '，采用两者较高值，未重复相加。'
    ];
    return '<div class="opb-sample-notes">' + [...lines, ...(sample.notes || [])].map(note => '<p>' + esc(note) + '</p>').join('') + '</div>';
  }
  const priorDetail = cxRenderSku;
  cxRenderSku = function () {
    const result = priorDetail.apply(this, arguments), sku = cxState.detail?.sku;
    const body = cxEl('cxDetailBody');
    if (!sku || !records.has(sku) || !body?.querySelector('.pp-section')) return result;
    if (!body.querySelector('.opb-evidence')) body.insertAdjacentHTML('afterbegin', evidenceHtml(sku, currentFilters()));
    // 将该订单的估算假设紧跟已有费用及原始来源，避免在主表堆长说明。
    const profit = model.forSku(sku, currentFilters());
    if (profit.ownBackfill) for (const row of body.querySelectorAll('.pp-samples-wrap tbody tr')) {
      const orderId = row.cells?.[1]?.childNodes?.[0]?.textContent?.trim();
      const sample = profit.samples.find(sample => orderId === sample.orderId);
      const detail = row.querySelector('details');
      if (sample && detail && !detail.querySelector('.opb-sample-notes')) detail.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend',
        sampleEvidenceHtml(sample));
    }
    return result;
  };
  const currentCsvName = name => name.replace(/_\d{8}(?=\.csv$|$)/, '_' + packet.asOf.replaceAll('-', ''));
  // 原库存补丁会在CSV链深处把文件名改回11/16；在实际保存入口统一本次日期。
  const priorManualDownload = downloadManual;
  downloadManual = function (value, name, type) {
    return priorManualDownload(value, String(name).startsWith('SKU数据查询_') ? currentCsvName(name) : name, type);
  };
  const priorExport = sqUiExport;
  sqUiExport = function () {
    const rows = sqExportRows({ ...sqUiState.filters }, { ...sqUiState.sort }), bySku = new Map(rows.map(row => [row.sku, row]));
    const priorDownload = cxDownloadCsv;
    cxDownloadCsv = function (name, headers, values) {
      if (!String(name).startsWith('SKU数据查询_')) return priorDownload(name, headers, values);
      const skuColumn = headers.indexOf('商品SKU');
      if (skuColumn < 0) throw Error('自身利润补证导出缺少SKU身份列');
      return priorDownload(currentCsvName(name),
        [...headers, '本轮自身利润补证', '自身补证缺项', '自身费用估算口径'], values.map(value => {
        const row = bySku.get(value[skuColumn]);
        if (!row) throw Error('自身利润导出与查询人口不一致');
        return [...value, row.profitBackfillStatus || '', row.profitMissingFields || '', row.profitEstimationNotes || ''];
      }));
    };
    try { return priorExport(); } finally { cxDownloadCsv = priorDownload; }
  };
  model.clearCaches(); root.spQueryPeriodModel?.clearCaches(); ldReferenceCache.clear();
  root.allSkuOwnProfitModel = { ready: true, asOf: packet.asOf, records, samplesBySku,
    summary: packet.summary, evidenceHtml, forSku: (sku, filters) => model.forSku(sku, filters) };
  if (cxState.view === 'query') sqRenderQuery();
})(window);

</script>
<script>(function(){if(!window.allSkuOwnProfitModel?.ready||!window.salesDateBoundsModel?.ready||!window.priceChangeFilterModel?.ready)throw Error('自身利润补证或既有筛选未就绪');var n=document.createElement('span');n.id='allSkuOwnProfitReady';n.hidden=true;document.body.appendChild(n);})();</script>
